- **hig-reviewer**: Read-only review of Edmund's writing and UI. Two passes — (1) writing, always: PR body drafts, sample CHANGELOG lines, and prose a diff adds (docs, skills, comments, UI strings) against the house register; (2) HIG, when the diff touches UI: menus, key equivalents, settings panes, toolbars, popovers, alerts, windows, accessibility, copy against Apple's Human Interface Guidelines. Invoked by /ship on every ship; also on request ("HIG check this branch", "review this writing"). Does not review logic, performance, Markdown rendering fidelity, or doc drift (that is doc-drift's).
